&lt;project_context>
# 项目: schema_semantic_constrained_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-16980
- SSH 连接命令: ssh westd-16980  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：P8c 实验收尾——获取详细数据 + 更新 registry + 删除 cron

### 背景
P8c FT SciERC latency 实验已完成（551/551 unconstrained + 551/551 constrained），overhead mean=10%, median=11.42%。需要获取详细数据并关闭 registry。

### 步骤 1：获取详细 timing 数据

SSH 到 westd-21461，读取报告：
```bash
cat /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/p8c_latency_report.json 2>/dev/null
# 如果不在这个路径：
find /root/ -name "p8c_latency_report*" 2>/dev/null
```

也获取 per-doc 统计：
```bash
# unconstrained 均值
python3 -c "
import json
lines = open('/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/p8c_timing_ft_unconstrained.jsonl').readlines()
times = [json.loads(l)['time_seconds'] if 'time_seconds' in json.loads(l) else json.loads(l).get('elapsed', json.loads(l).get('time', 0)) for l in lines]
print(f'unconstrained: mean={sum(times)/len(times):.4f}s, median={sorted(times)[len(times)//2]:.4f}s, n={len(times)}')
" 2>/dev/null

# constrained 均值
python3 -c "
import json
lines = open('/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/p8c_timing_ft_constrained.jsonl').readlines()
times = [json.loads(l)['time_seconds'] if 'time_seconds' in json.loads(l) else json.loads(l).get('elapsed', json.loads(l).get('time', 0)) for l in lines]
print(f'constrained: mean={sum(times)/len(times):.4f}s, median={sorted(times)[len(times)//2]:.4f}s, n={len(times)}')
" 2>/dev/null
```

如果字段名不对，先看一行：
```bash
head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/p8c_timing_ft_unconstrained.jsonl
head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/p8c_timing_ft_constrained.jsonl
```

### 步骤 2：更新 registry

用获取到的实际 s/doc 数据更新 metrics：
```python
update_registry(exp_id="p8c_ft_scierc_latency", field="metrics", value={
    "unconstrained_mean_s": &lt;actual>,
    "constrained_mean_s": &lt;actual>,
    "unconstrained_median_s": &lt;actual>,
    "constrained_median_s": &lt;actual>,
    "overhead_mean_pct": 10.0,
    "overhead_median_pct": 11.42,
    "num_docs": 551
})
update_registry(exp_id="p8c_ft_scierc_latency", field="conclusion", value="success")
update_registry(exp_id="p8c_ft_scierc_latency", field="status", value="DONE")
```

注意：registry 状态可能在 PREPARING，需要逐步推进到 DONE。如果状态机不允许直接跳，尝试：
- PREPARING → 直接设 conclusion 和 checkpoint_path/log_path 后设 DONE
- 或者逐步 PREPARING → RUNNING (需要 spec) → ANALYZING → DONE

如果遇到阻碍，至少确保 metrics 和 conclusion 写入。

### 步骤 3：删除 cron

```python
delete_cron("p8c_ft_scierc_latency")
```

### 汇报
```
unconstrained: mean=Xs/doc, median=Xs/doc
constrained: mean=Xs/doc, median=Xs/doc
overhead: mean=X%, median=X%
registry: DONE(success)
cron: deleted
```

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching.
